Observation: The objective T_n is scale-invariant and **subadditive/composable**: If we have a block sequence m of length K and expand each entry by W equal weights, a length KW sequence has the same score T_K exactly. Therefore the best score for length N can be as good as best score for any divisor K of N (or by rounding). Thus we can restrict to sequences that are piecewise constant blocks of sizes W possibly. Let's design code with two tracks:
A) **Block track**:
- For each K in [64,128,256,512,1024] maybe (if time), optimize m length K with L-BFGS using smooth objective. Expand best to N (multiple by W=N/K if divides; if not choose K dividing N, all powers of 2). We can also use lengths that divide 8192: powers of 2. For score maybe best at largest K we can optimize within budget. K=1024 with L-BFGS is easy (FFT 2048) even thousands iterations. Could find maybe 1.51? Need enough global starts.
- Parameterize log probabilities with last fixed. For small K, could even use larger smooth tau, multi-start.
- Multi-start initializations:
   * uniform.
   * monotone decreasing linear/exponential: p_i ∝ r^i with r random [0.3,0.9]? K=2 optimum r=0.5. Try r in [0.3,0.8]. For K=3 best first ratio .5, then .75; maybe two-parameter shape.
   * polynomial/Chebyshev random.
   * previous best compressed/resized to K.
   * "solitonal" from solving K by Adam.
   * random sorted m: generate random log weights, sort descending? Since optimal likely monotone decreasing. Use monotone start: p ∝ exp(-β x) + noise, then sort descending? Sorting may disrupt convolution but okay as start.
- L-BFGS with smooth tau schedule: tau_ratio 0.5,0.2,0.05,0.01 coefficient units? For small K, M ~score/(2K). We can use tau = 0.1*M. L-BFGS maxiter maybe 1000. Runtime small.
- After finding m, expand to N: each entry repeated W=N/K. Evaluate fast_score. This final expanded sequence will have score exactly T_K (within FFT numerical) if N exactly multiple. Use it for candidate.

B) **Full track**:
- Start from previous best resized to N=8192 or 16384, and from best block expanded.
- Use L-BFGS/Adam with fewer iterations to improve. Because L-BFGS maybe improves beyond block.

Now, can we reason about L-BFGS implementation details.

Top-level functions:
- `next_pow2(x)`.
- `safe_positive_array(arr)`.
- `normalize_positive(a)`.
- `fast_score(a)`: as before linear convolution via FFT. To avoid allocating too large? n up to 16384 => L=next_pow2(2n-1) for score (only need p*p, length 4n? Wait linear conv length 2n-1, L must be ≥2n-1. For score only, L=next_pow2(2n-1). For gradient, L=next_pow2(3n-2) to handle cross-correlation with weights length 2n-1? We can use same for both; for n=16384, L=65536. OK. But if scoring only, next_pow2(2n-1) enough, but gradient needs 3n-2. Use one function.
- `smooth_max_info(p, tau, L=False)`: returns c, M, smooth coefficient, weights w (length m=2n-1) maybe. To save allocation, compute each call.
- `smooth_objective(x, tau, n)`: x length n-1 (relative log with last fixed). Build z length n: z[:n-1]=x, z[n-1]=0. p=softmax(z). Compute c, M, value. Return 2n*value. Need if tau too small? Use M. Could compute tau per call from objective? We can pass fixed tau. But objective changes; we can define `smooth_objective_dynamic(x, tau_ratio, n)` where tau=tau_ratio * max(1e-30, M) within the call? But then objective non-smooth at M? tau based on M makes gradient require M derivative? We can just compute M then tau, but objective becomes τ * log... with τ depending on p, gradient not correct. So use fixed tau per minimize call. We can choose tau from initial M: `tau = tau_ratio * M_initial` (M_initial cmax coefficient). In objective call, use that tau.
- `smooth_grad_value(x, tau, n)`: compute value + gradient. Build z, p. Compute c, M. If tau <=0 maybe exact? Use smooth with max(tau,1e-300). value_coeff = M + tau logsumexp((c-M)/tau). But for small tau, exp. We can optimize by only considering top K? Not necessary for n 1024/8192; c length ~2K.
   We can improve speed: To compute logsumexp stable: z=(c-M)/tau; shift by max=0? Since M max, (c-M)≤0. expz ≤1. sum ≥1. No overflow. Underflow okay. If tau very small and many c close, sum could be near count near max; okay. Use z = np.clip((c-M)/tau, -50,0) to avoid underflow? Underflow to 0 ok. But if M is max, no positive. Use `z = (c - M) / tau`; if tau >0 else. `expz = np.exp(np.maximum(z, -700))`. Sum.
   weights w = expz / sum. ∂value_coeff/∂c = expz/sum. (Not include tau? value=M+tau log sum; derivative wrt c_k = δ_{max}? With soft, derivative = p_k. Since M is max of c not variable? In smooth max formula cm + tau logsumexp((c-cm)/tau) if cm = max(c), derivative? Standard softmax over (c-cm) with cm as subtract constant: derivative wrt c_k = p_k, because derivative of logsumexp = p_k and derivative of cm? If cm is argmax and included? Actually smooth max = max(c)+tau log∑ exp((c_i-max)/tau). If max changes discretely, derivative of max is 1 for active max; but the standard smooth max using `m0 = max(c)` and `tau logsumexp((c-m0)/tau)` derivative is p_k if m0 is also treated as a variable? Need implement monotone initialization from p:
```
def p_to_monotone_log_gaps(p):
    p = np.asarray(p)
    p = np.maximum(p, 0)
    s = p.sum()
    if s==0: p uniform
    p = p/s
    # if not nonincreasing, sort descending? Since param requires nonincreasing. For warm start, sort descending may change score. For evaluation candidates, we can use sorted p separately. For param init, sort descending to represent closest monotone shape.
    p_sorted = np.sort(p)[::-1]
    n=len
    e = np.empty(n)
    e[:-1] = p_sorted[:-1] - p_sorted[1:]
    e[-1] = p_sorted[-1]
    e = np.maximum(e, 1e-15)
    return np.log(e)
```

Let's design monotone L-BFGS function:
```
def run_monotone_lbfgs(p, budget, rng, tau_ratios=(0.3,0.1,0.03,0.008), min_maxfun=80, cap_maxfun=5000, disp_label="")
    p = normalize_positive(p)
    if p is None: return p, inf
    best_p=p; best_s=fast_score(p)
    t_start=time.monotonic()
    for ti, ratio in enumerate(tau_ratios):
       rem=budget-(time.monotonic()-t_start)
       if rem < 2: break
       # allocate stage budget: maybe rem / (len(tau_ratios)-stage)
       stage_budget = rem / max(1, len(tau_ratios)-stage)
       if stage_budget < 1: break
       n=len(p); L=next_pow2(3*n-2)
       x0=gaps_from_p(p)
       M=best_s/(2.0*n)
       tau=max(1e-300, ratio*M)
       # time eval
       t0=time.monotonic(); value, grad=monotone_f_and_grad(x0,tau,n,L); t_eval=time.monotonic()-t0
       maxfun = int(min(cap_maxfun, max(min_maxfun, stage_budget/(max(1e-5,t_eval*2.0)))))
       # maybe if maxfun<50 break
       res = minimize(monotone_f_and_grad, x0, args=(tau,n,L), method='L-BFGS-B', jac=True, options={'maxfun':maxfun,'ftol':1e-12,'gtol':1e-7,'maxiter':maxfun*2? but maxfun in options means max function evaluations? Scipy uses maxfun. Set maxiter=10000 to not limit.})
       if res.x finite:
          p_new=gaps_to_p(res.x)
          s=fast_score(p_new)
          if finite and s<best_s: best...
       else: p_new=p
    return best_p,best_s
```
But L-BFGS options `maxfun` is maximum number of function evaluations. If we set `maxiter` default 1000*(n?)? Default okay? Set `'maxiter': max(1000, maxfun)` to not stop early. But `maxfun` will stop. `disp`: False.

How to generate random monotone p (descending) directly:
```
def random_monotone_p(n, rng, type):
   if type=0:
      beta = rng.uniform(0.0, 4.0)
      p = np.exp(-beta * np.linspace(0,1,n))
   elif type=1:
      alpha = rng.uniform(0.1, 3.0)
      p = 1.0/(np.arange(n)+1.0)**alpha
   elif type=2:
      # exponential gaps: logs = -beta*i + noise
      beta=rng.uniform(0.0,3.0)
      z=-beta*i + rng.normal(0,0.2,n)
      e=np.exp(z.clip(-10,10))
      e[-1]=1? if p from cumsum; normalize.
   normalize
   sort descending maybe.
```
For cumsum, p_i = ∑_{m=i}^{n-1} e_m. If e_m decays with m, p maybe decreasing. Random φ with negative drift: x_m = -beta*m + noise; x_last fix? If x_last maybe not 1 but fine for p. Use `gaps_to_p` for initial before L-BFGS: set x_last to one? We can just construct e positive and cumsum.
```
xg = rng.normal(0,0.5,n-1) - beta*np.arange(n-1)
e=concat(exp(clip(xg)),[1])
a=cumsum(e[::-1])[::-1]
p=normalize(a)
```
This yields monotone.

Let's verify gradient for monotone with scale invariance. In `monotone_f_and_grad`, after computing g_p (gradient of value_coeff wrt p) multiply by 2n to F gradient. No S derivative separately because S is function of a in F but accounted. Good.

Then for p constrained sum1, gradient projected. We do projection. Good.

Now, `smooth_max_gradient`:
```
def smooth_max_gradient(p, tau, L):
    n=p.size; P=rfft(p,L); c=irfft(P*P,L)[:2*n-1]; c=...
    M=max
    z=(c-M)/tau; expz...
    weights=expz/sum
    value = M + tau*log(sum)
    Wref=weights[::-1]
    H=irfft(P*rfft(Wref,L), L)
    grad=2*H[n-1:2*n-1][::-1]
    return value, grad
```
Wait if weights sum not 1? weights probability sum1. Good. But the derivative of `tau logsumexp(c/tau)` is weights (probability). We subtracted M for stability. The value is M+tau logsum. If tau small, value close M.
Potential issue with c negative tiny: clip 0. If all c zero? no.

`fast_score` can use same but exact max; L=next_pow2(2n-1) enough. To avoid negative, clip.

Implement `normalize_positive`:
```
def normalize_positive(a):
    a=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size==0: return None
    a=np.where(np.isfinite(a),a,0.0)
    a=np.maximum(a,0.0)
    s=float(a.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s<=0: return None
    return a/s
```

`fast_score` with L=next_pow2(2n-1) for score:
```
L=1 << (2*n-2).bit_length()? next_pow2(2*n-1)
F=rfft(p,L); c=irfft(F*F,L)[:2*n-1]
return 2n*max
```
If n=1: conv [p0^2]=1, score=2. `next_pow2(1)=1`; rfft length1 ok? rfft of length1 returns [p0]. irfft returns length1? `irfft(F*F, 1)`? length 1 okay. But grading likely n>1. Handle n=1 return2.

`next_pow2(x)`:
```
x=int(max(1,x)); return 1 << (x-1).bit_length()

`monotone_f_and_grad`:
```
def monotone_f_and_grad(x, tau, n, L):
    x=np.asarray(x,dtype=np.float64)
    e=np.ones(n,dtype=np.float64)
    e[:-1]=np.exp(np.clip(x, -40.0, 40.0))
    # If x large, e huge but okay; S maybe overflow if exp40*16k ~1e18*16k=1e22 <1e308 okay.
    a=np.cumsum(e[::-1])[::-1]
    S=float(a.sum())
    if not finite or S<=0: return 1e12, np.zeros_like(x)
    p=a/S
    vc, gc=smooth_max_gradient(p,tau,L)
    if not finite: return 1e12, zeros
    val=2.0*n*vc
    gp=2.0*n*gc
    mean=float(np.dot(p, gp))
    grad_a=(gp - p*mean)/S
    cum=np.cumsum(grad_a)
    grad=e[:-1]*cum[:-1]
    grad=np.where(np.isfinite(grad), grad, 0.0)
    # Optional scale if max grad too large? L-BFGS may handle. Could clip to 1e3/ n? maybe.
    mg=max_abs=np.linalg.norm? not.
    return float(val), grad
```
Wait `p=a/S`, but `e` positive normalized? If x all within ±40, S may be huge but finite. `gp` gradient wrt p of value_coeff times 2n. `grad_a` small? If S huge, grad_a small; grad_phi = e* cum, but cum includes /S, e maybe huge; product may be huge? Should be O(1). Let's test scaling: If multiply all e by C, a,C, S,C, p same, grad_a scales 1/C, e scales C, grad_phi invariant. Good.

`free_f_and_grad`:
```
z=zeros(n); z[:-1]=x; z[-1]=0
z-=z.max(); z=clip(z,-50,50)
e=exp(z); p=e/e.sum()
vc,gc=...
val=2n*vc; gp=2n*gc; mean=dot(p,gp); grad_full=gp - p*mean; grad=grad_full[:-1]
```
Maybe clip grad max to avoid overflow.

`p_to_gaps(p)`:

p=normalize_positive(p)
n=p.size
p_sorted=np.sort(p)[::-1]
# If n=1: return empty
e=np.empty(n)
e[:-1]=p_sorted[:-1]-p_sorted[1:]
e[-1]=p_sorted[-1]
e=np.maximum(e,1e-15)
# To keep x inside, scale by max? We can set base = max(e[-1], 1e-12*float(e.mean())?) but need e[-1] as reference. If last very small, ratios huge; clip.
base=float(e[-1])
if base <= 1e-300: base=1e-12
x=np.log(np.maximum(e[:-1],1e-300)/base)
return np.clip(x, -40.0, 40.0)
```
If p_sorted last > some e gaps, x negative okay.

```

Random monotone:
```
def random_monotone_p(n, rng, kind):
  x=np.linspace(0,1,n)
  if kind==0: beta=rng.uniform(0.0,6.0); p=np.exp(-beta*x)
  elif kind==1: alpha=rng.uniform(0.1,4.0); p=1.0/(np.arange(n)+1)**alpha
  elif kind==2:
     beta=rng.uniform(0.0,3.0)
     idx=np.arange(n)
     z=-beta*idx + rng.normal(0,0.5,n)
     e=np.exp(np.clip(z,-10,10)); e[-1]=1.0? maybe set e[-1]=exp(clip(z[-1]));
     a=np.cumsum(e[::-1])[::-1]; p=a/a.sum()
  elif kind==3: # two level
     split=rng.uniform(0.3,0.7)*n
     high=rng.uniform(1.0,3.0); low=rng.uniform(0.0,1.0)
     p=np.where(np.arange(n)<split, high, low); if low=0 okay
  normalize and sort desc (some kind might not sorted; sort to ensure monotone)
```
For kind 3 with step, if low high, sort handles maybe.

`random_cheb_p` maybe for free? We can include if free starts need diversity. But monotone starts enough.

`run_monotone_lbfgs` details with benchmark:
```
def run_monotone_lbfgs(p, time_budget, tau_ratios=(0.3,0.1,0.03,0.01), label=""):
    p=normalize_positive(p)
    if p is None: return None,np.inf
    best_p=p; best_s=fast_score(p)
    start= time.monotonic()
    n=len(p); L=next_pow2(3*n-2)
    x0=p_to_gaps(p)
    # measure eval
    M0=best_s/(2.0*n)
    tau0=max(1e-12, tau_ratios[0]*M0)
    t0=time.monotonic(); val,grad=monotone_f_and_grad(x0,tau0,n,L); teval=time.monotonic()-t0
    bounds=[(-40.0,40.0)]*(n-1)
    opts_base={'maxiter':10000,'ftol':1e-12,'gtol':1e-6,'disp':False,'maxcor':10}
    for stage,ratio in enumerate(tau_ratios):
       elapsed=time.monotonic()-start
       rem=time_budget-elapsed
       if rem<2 or not HAVE_MINIMIZE: break
       nstages_left=len(tau_ratios)-stage
       stage_budget=rem / nstages_left
       if stage_budget<1: stage_budget=1
       tau=max(1e-12, ratio*best_s/(2*n))
       # maybe if tau too small relative M? fine
       t0=... maybe reuse teval from initial? Could vary n no. Use previous teval.
       maxfun=int(min(3000, max(60, stage_budget/(max(1e-6, teval*1.5)))))
       opts=dict(opts_base); opts['maxfun']=maxfun
       try:
          res=minimize(monotone_f_and_grad, x0, args=(tau,n,L), method='L-BFGS-B', jac=True, bounds=bounds, options=opts)
       except Exception: break
       if hasattr(res,'x'):
          x_new=np.asarray(res.x); if finite: p_new=gaps_to_p(x_new); s=fast_score(p_new); if finite and s<best_s: best...; x0=x_new
       # update teval maybe measure at x_new?
       if time.monotonic()-start > time_budget: break
    return best_p,best_s
```
But `bounds` list creation may be expensive but OK. For n=1? If n=1, x empty. Handle separately: return p,best_s.
`teval` measured before possibly x0 far; use average? fine.
The benchmark `val,grad` itself is a function call; if n small, teval may be very small; maxfun cap.
Potential: If L-BFGS fails improving in first stage due to tau too large, next stage uses best_s (unchanged) for tau, same ratio smaller. Good.
Could update p to result even if fast_score not improved but smooth value improved? Fast score true might not improve much; but for next stage use p from res if finite? We can use p_new if finite even if score same, to continue sharp. But if score worse, avoid. Maybe use if `res.fun < best_s`? `res.fun` is smooth not true. Could use p_new if fast_score <= best_s + 1e-6; but to avoid jumping up, use only if fast_score finite and (s < best_s + 1e-5). But more aggressive: keep best by true score, but for p for next stage, if s < best_s*1.02 maybe use p_new (more sharp). Simpler only if improved. Multistart handles.

`consider` top-level update:
```
def update_candidate(q, N, best_p, best_s):
    q=normalize_positive(q)
    if q is None: return best_p,best_s
    if q.size != N: q=resize_sequence(q,N)
    s=fast_score(q)
    if s < best_s:
        return q, s
    return best_p,best_s
```

Let's define `update_candidate` top-level returning tuple.
```
def update_candidate(p, N, best_p, best_s):
   pq=normalize_positive(p)
   if pq is None: return best_p,best_s
   if pq.size != N: pq=resize_sequence(pq,N)
   if pq is None: return
   s=fast_score(pq)
   if np.isfinite(s) and s < best_s:
       return pq, s
   return best_p,best_s
```
We can also update with sorted:
```
def consider_sorted(p,N,best...): (p, sorted) both.
```
But to avoid nested, in propose just call update_candidate twice.

Detailed `run_monotone_lbfgs` with `time_budget`: If budget <=0 return.
```
def run_monotone_lbfgs(p, time_budget, tau_ratios=None, label=""):
    if tau_ratios is None: tau_ratios=(0.3,0.1,0.03,0.01)
    p=normalize_positive(p)
    if p is None: return None,np.inf
    n=p.size
    if n<=1: return p,2.0
    best_p=p.copy(); best_s=fast_score(p)
    start=time.monotonic()
    L=next_pow2(3*n-2)
    x0=p_to_gaps(p)
    M0=best_s/(2*n)
    if not finite: return
    # benchmark
    tau=tau_ratios[0]*M0
    teval=1e-4
    if HAVE_MINIMIZE:
       t0=time.monotonic(); _=monotone_f_and_grad(x0,tau,n,L); teval=time.monotonic()-t0
    teval=max(teval,1e-6)
    bounds=[(-40.0,40.0)]*(n-1)
    for stage, ratio in enumerate(tau_ratios):
       rem=time_budget-(time.monotonic()-start)
       if rem<3 or not HAVE_MINIMIZE: break
       nleft=len(tau_ratios)-stage
       stage_budget=rem / nleft
       if stage_budget<2: stage_budget=2
       tau=max(1e-15, ratio*best_s/(2*n))
       maxfun=int(min(3000, max(60, stage_budget/(max(1e-6,teval*1.5)))))
       opts={'maxfun':maxfun,'maxiter':max(1000, maxfun+100),'ftol':1e-12,'gtol':1e-6,'maxcor':10,'disp':False}
       try:
          res=minimize(monotone_f_and_grad, x0, args=(tau,n,L), method='L-BFGS-B', jac=True, bounds=bounds, options=opts)
       except Exception:
          break
       if hasattr(res, 'x'):
          x_new=np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64)
          if np.all(np.isfinite(x_new)):
             p_new=gaps_to_p(x_new)
             if p_new is not None:
                s_new=fast_score(p_new)
                if np.isfinite(s_new) and s_new < best_s + 1e-8:
                   if s_new < best_s: best_s=s_new
                   best_p=p_new.copy(); x0=x_new
       # optional print
       if stage % max(1,len(tau_ratios)//3)==0: print(label, 'stage', stage, 'score', best_s, 'time', ...)
    return best_p,best_s
```

`random_monotone_p` ensure always returns normalized sorted:
```
def random_monotone_p(n, rng, kind=-1):
   if kind<0: kind=int(rng.integers(0,5))
   idx=np.arange(n,dtype=np.float64)
   if n==1: return np.ones(1)
   t=idx/(n-1)
   if kind==0: T=rng.uniform(0.2,20.0); p=np.exp(-T*t)
   elif kind==1: alpha=rng.uniform(0.1,5.0); p=1.0/(idx+1.0)**alpha
   elif kind==2:
       drift=rng.uniform(-5.0,15.0) # negative drift positive decreases; can be positive increasing but sort
       noise=0.5*rng.standard_normal(n)
       phi=drift*t + noise
       e=np.exp(np.clip(phi,-8,8))
       a=np.cumsum(e[::-1])[::-1]
       p=a/a.sum()
   elif kind==3:
       split=int(rng.integers(max(1,n//4), n*3//4+1))
       hi=float(rng.uniform(0.5,5.0)); lo=float(rng.uniform(0.0, hi))
       p=np.empty(n); p[:split]=hi; p[split:]=lo
       if p.sum()==0: p=np.ones(n)
   else:
       a=rng.uniform(0.5,10); b=rng.uniform(0.5,10)
       p=np.exp(-a*t)+0.5*np.exp(-b*t)
   p=normalize_positive(p)
   if p is None: p=np.ones(n)/n
   p=np.sort(np.asarray(p,dtype=np.float64))[::-1]
   return p
```
Kind 3 if lo>hi? we set lo < hi. If hi random less? no.
For kind 2 drift positive might increase; sort handles. Good.

`next_pow2`:
```
def next_pow2(x):
    x=int(x)
    if x <=1: return 1
    return 1 << (x-1).bit_length()
```

`normalize_positive` as above.

`fast_score`:
```
def fast_score(a):
    p=normalize_positive(a)
    if p is None: return np.inf
    n=p.size
    if n<=0: return np.inf
    if n==1: return 2.0
    L=next_pow2(2*n-1)
    F=np.fft.rfft(p,L)
    c=np.fft.irfft(F*F,L)[:2*n-1]
    c=np.where(np.isfinite(c), c, 0.0)
    cm=float(np.max(c))
    if not np.isfinite(cm) or cm<=0.0: return np.inf
    return float(2.0*n*cm)
```

`smooth_max_gradient`:
```
def smooth_max_gradient(p, tau, L):
    n=p.size
    if n<=1:
       return 1.0, np.zeros(n)
    P=np.fft.rfft(p,L)
    c=np.fft.irfft(P*P,L)[:2*n-1]
    c=np.where(np.isfinite(c), c, 0.0)
    c=np.maximum(c, 0.0)
    M=float(np.max(c))
    if not np.isfinite(M) or M<=0.0:
       return np.inf, np.zeros(n)
    if tau is None or tau <=0 or not np.isfinite(tau):
       tau=1e-12
    z=(c-M)/tau
    # if tau very small, z negative huge; clip
    z=np.maximum(z, -745.0)
    expz=np.exp(z)
    s=float(expz.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s<=0.0:
       expz=np.zeros_like(c); expz[int(np.argmax(c))]=1.0; s=1.0
    w=expz/s
    val=M + tau*float(np.log(s))
    wr=w[::-1]
    H=np.fft.irfft(P*np.fft.rfft(wr,L), L)
    g=2.0*H[n-1:2*n-1][::-1]
    g=np.where(np.isfinite(g), g, 0.0)
    return float(val), g
```
For n=1, value_coeff? Uniform sum1 p=[1], c=[1], max=1, score 2. But run skip. Return.

`monotone_f_and_grad`:
```
def monotone_f_and_grad(x, tau, n, L):
    n=int(n)
    x=np.asarray(x,dtype=np.float64)
    if x.size != n-1:
       # try trim
       if x.size < n-1: x=np.concatenate([x, np.zeros(n-1-x.size)])
       else: x=x[:n-1]
    if n<=1:
       return 2.0, np.zeros(0)
    e=np.ones(n,dtype=np.float64)
    if n>1:
       e[:-1]=np.exp(np.clip(x, -40.0, 40.0))
    a=np.cumsum(e[::-1])[::-1]
    S=float(a.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(S) or S<=0.0:
       return 1e12, np.zeros(n-1)
    p=a/S
    vc,gc=smooth_max_gradient(p,tau,L)
    if not np.isfinite(vc):
       return 1e12, np.zeros(n-1)
    val=2.0*n*vc
    gp=2.0*n*gc
    mean=float(np.dot(p,gp))
    grad_a=(gp - p*mean)/S
    cum=np.cumsum(grad_a)
    grad=e[:-1]*cum[:-1]
    grad=np.where(np.isfinite(grad), grad, 0.0)
    # optional scale to avoid huge
    mg=float(np.max(np.abs(grad)))
    if np.isfinite(mg) and mg > 1e5:
       grad *= (1e5/mg)
    return float(val), grad
```
`n<=1` returns grad zeros length 0. `minimize` with x0 empty and bounds empty? We skip.

`free_f_and_grad`:
```
def free_f_and_grad(x, tau, n, L):
   n=int(n); x=... length n-1
   if n<=1: return 2.0, zeros0
   z=np.zeros(n); z[:-1]=x
   zmax=float(np.max(z)); z=z-zmax
   z=np.clip(z, -50.0, 50.0)
   en=np.exp(z)
   s=float(en.sum())
   p=en/s if s>0 else np.ones(n)/n
   vc,gc=smooth...
   val=2n*vc
   gp=2n*gc
   mean=dot(p,gp)
   grad_full=gp - p*mean
   grad=grad_full[:-1]
   clip if huge
   return val,grad
```
No need baseline last fixed? Last z=0 initially, but `z-=zmax` changes last not fixed? Wait after `z=z-zmax`, the representation has all coordinates shifted; softmax unchanged. But parameter x fixed last=0 only before shift? In free parametrization, we set last coordinate to 0, then subtract max for numeric. The resulting p is same as softmax of original. The gradient w.r.t x earlier assumed z = [x,0] without subtracting max? Subtracting a constant adds to all components; gradient transformation should still be same because softmax invariant. Our gradient formula using p from shifted z and `grad_full[:-1]` should be valid for x because p depends only on relative differences. However if we subtract max, the actual z vector used is z_shift = [x,0]-max. The mapping x->p is same. The gradient computed as `g_p - p mean` with last fixed? Does the flat direction projection remain? Yes, p function of x. The derivative of p wrt x is p_i(δ_i - p_j?) For j<n-1. With last fixed value 0 (before numeric shift), but numeric shift is just algorithm; the mathematical model is [x,0]. So gradient full wrt all n variables (if last free) would be g_p - p mean; returning first n-1 components is gradient wrt fixed baseline. Subtracting max in code changes z but p same. The gradient formula based on p only is correct; it does not depend on shift. Good.
But when x components large, zmax shift may make last not 0 but p same.

`p_to_gaps` as above. Ensure if base underflow:
```
def p_to_gaps(p):
   p=normalize_positive(p)
   n=p.size
   if n<=1: return np.zeros(0)
   ps=np.sort(p)[::-1]
   e=np.empty(n)
   e[:-1]=ps[:-1]-ps[1:]
   e[-1]=ps[-1]
   e=np.where(np.isfinite(e),e,0.0)
   e=np.maximum(e,1e-15)
   base=float(e[-1])
   if base<=1e-300: base=e.mean()
   if base<=1e-300: base=1e-12
   x=np.log(e[:-1]/base)
   return np.clip(x,-40.0,40.0)
```
If e[:-1]/base >1e17, clip later.

def gaps_to_p(x):
   n= len(x)+1
   e=ones(n); e[:-1]=exp(clip(x,-40,40))
   a=cumsum(e[::-1])[::-1]
   return normalize_positive(a)
```

`multi_start_monotone`:
```
def multi_start_monotone(K, base_p, rng, time_budget, nstarts=None, label=""):
   if not HAVE_MINIMIZE:
       # just score starts
   t0=time.monotonic()
   if nstarts is None: nstarts=max(4, min(30, 2000//max(1,K//16)))
   starts=make_monotone_starts(K, nstarts, base_p, rng)
   # score starts and order
   scored=[]
   for p in starts:
       q=normalize_positive(p)
       if q is None: continue
       s=fast_score(q)
       if np.isfinite(s): scored.append((s,q))
   if not scored: return None,np.inf
   scored.sort(key=lambda t:t[0]) # lambda not allowed! They said no lambda functions. Need avoid lambda. Use `scored.sort()` which sorts by tuple (score,p) but p array in tuple comparison if scores equal may compare arrays causing error? Python tuple with second element array may compare arrays? If scores equal, it will try compare arrays, which returns array bool and error. We need sort without lambda and without comparing arrays. We can sort indices by score using numpy? `order=np.array(sorted(range(len(scored)), key=lambda i: scored[i][0]))` lambda not allowed. Could avoid by not sorting; deterministic order okay. def better_candidate(p, N, best_p, best_s):
    q=normalize_positive(p)
    if q is None: return best_p,best_s
    if q.size != N: q=resize_sequence(q,N)
    if q is None: return best_p,best_s
    s=fast_score(q)
    if np.isfinite(s) and s < best_s:
       return q, s
    return best_p,best_s
```

```python
"""
Search for a non-negative coefficient sequence minimizing

    2 * len(a) * max(conv(a, a)) / (sum(a) ** 2)

using:
- a fast FFT implementation of the same scale-invariant objective,
- multi-start L-BFGS optimization in a monotone-decreasing cumulative-gap
  parametrization,
- several intermediate lengths and exact block expansion,
- a free log-domain L-BFGS phase for non-monotone escape,
- final validation against evaluate_sequence when available.
"""

import time
import numpy as np

try:
    from scipy.optimize import minimize
    HAVE_MINIMIZE = True
except Exception:
    minimize = None
    HAVE_MINIMIZE = False


def next_pow2(x):
    x = int(x)
    if x <= 1:
        return 1
    return 1 << (x - 1).bit_length()


def normalize_positive(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size == 0:
        return None
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
        return None
    return a / s


def fast_score(a):
    p = normalize_positive(a)
    if p is None:
        return np.inf
    n = p.size
    if n <= 0:
        return np.inf
    if n == 1:
        return 2.0
    L = next_pow2(2 * n - 1)
    F = np.fft.rfft(p, L)
    c = np.fft.irfft(F * F, L)[:2 * n - 1]
    c = np.where(np.isfinite(c), c, 0.0)
    cm = float(np.max(c))
    if not np.isfinite(cm) or cm <= 0.0:
        return np.inf
    return float(2.0 * n * cm)


def smooth_max_gradient(p, tau, L):
    n = p.size
    if n <= 1:
        return 1.0, np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)

    P = np.fft.rfft(p, L)
    c = np.fft.irfft(P * P, L)[:2 * n - 1]
    c = np.where(np.isfinite(c), c, 0.0)
    c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)

    M = float(np.max(c))
    if not np.isfinite(M) or M <= 0.0:
        return np.inf, np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)

    if tau is None or tau <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(tau):
        tau = 1e-12

    z = (c - M) / tau
    z = np.maximum(z, -745.0)
    expz = np.exp(z)
    s = float(expz.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        expz = np.zeros_like(c)
        expz[int(np.argmax(c))] = 1.0
        s = 1.0

    w = expz / s
    value = M + tau * float(np.log(s))

    wr = w[::-1]
    H = np.fft.irfft(P * np.fft.rfft(wr, L), L)
    g = 2.0 * H[n - 1:2 * n - 1][::-1]
    g = np.where(np.isfinite(g), g, 0.0)

    return float(value), g


def monotone_f_and_grad(x, tau, n, L):
    n = int(n)
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64)

    if x.size != n - 1:
        if x.size < n - 1:
            x = np.concatenate((x, np.zeros(n - 1 - x.size, dtype=np.float64)))
        else:
            x = x[:n - 1]

    if n <= 1:
        return 2.0, np.zeros(0, dtype=np.float64)

    e = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
    e[:-1] = np.exp(np.clip(x, -40.0, 40.0))

    a = np.cumsum(e[::-1])[::-1]
    S = float(a.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(S) or S <= 1e-300:
        return 1e12, np.zeros(n - 1, dtype=np.float64)

    p = a / S
    vc, gc = smooth_max_gradient(p, tau, L)
    if not np.isfinite(vc):
        return 1e12, np.zeros(n - 1, dtype=np.float64)

    value = 2.0 * n * vc
    gp = 2.0 * n * gc
    mean_g = float(np.dot(p, gp))

    grad_a = (gp - p * mean_g) / S
    cum = np.cumsum(grad_a)
    grad = e[:-1] * cum[:-1]
    grad = np.where(np.isfinite(grad), grad, 0.0)

    mg = float(np.max(np.abs(grad))) if grad.size else 0.0
    if np.isfinite(mg) and mg > 1e5:
        grad *= (1e5 / mg)

    return float(value), grad


def free_f_and_grad(x, tau, n, L):
    n = int(n)
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64)

    if x.size != n - 1:
        if x.size < n - 1:
            x = np.concatenate((x, np.zeros(n - 1 - x.size, dtype=np.float64)))
        else:
            x = x[:n - 1]

    if n <= 1:
        return 2.0, np.zeros(0, dtype=np.float64)

    z = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    z[:-1] = x
    zmax = float(np.max(z))
    z = z - zmax
    z = np.clip(z, -50.0, 50.0)

    en = np.exp(z)
    s = float(en.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
        p = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
    else:
        p = en / s

    vc, gc = smooth_max_gradient(p, tau, L)
    if not np.isfinite(vc):
        return 1e12, np.zeros(n - 1, dtype=np.float64)

    value = 2.0 * n * vc
    gp = 2.0 * n * gc
    mean_g = float(np.dot(p, gp))

    grad_full = gp - p * mean_g
    grad = grad_full[:-1]
    grad = np.where(np.isfinite(grad), grad, 0.0)

    mg = float(np.max(np.abs(grad))) if grad.size else 0.0
    if np.isfinite(mg) and mg > 1e5:
        grad *= (1e5 / mg)

    return float(value), grad


def gaps_to_p(x):
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = x.size + 1
    if n <= 0:
        return None
    e = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
    if x.size:
        e[:-1] = np.exp(np.clip(x, -40.0, 40.0))
    a = np.cumsum(e[::-1])[::-1]
    return normalize_positive(a)


def p_to_gaps(p):
    p = normalize_positive(p)
    if p is None:
        n = 1
        return np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64) if n > 1 else np.zeros(0, dtype=np.float64)

    n = p.size
    if n <= 1:
        return np.zeros(0, dtype=np.float64)

    ps = np.sort(p)[::-1]
    e = np.empty(n, dtype=np.float64)
    e[:-1] = ps[:-1] - ps[1:]
    e[-1] = ps[-1]
    e = np.where(np.isfinite(e), e, 0.0)
    e = np.maximum(e, 1e-15)

    base = float(e[-1])
    if base <= 1e-300:
        base = float(e.mean())
    if base <= 1e-300:
        base = 1e-12

    x = np.log(e[:-1] / base)
    return np.clip(x, -40.0, 40.0)


def free_x_to_p(x):
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = x.size + 1
    z = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    if x.size:
        z[:-1] = x
    zmax = float(np.max(z))
    z = np.clip(z - zmax, -50.0, 50.0)
    en = np.exp(z)
    s = float(en.sum())
    if s <= 0.0:
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
    return normalize_positive(en / s)


def p_to_free_x(p):
    p = normalize_positive(p)
    n = max(1, p.size if p is not None else 1)
    if p is None:
        return np.zeros(max(0, n - 1), dtype=np.float64)
    z = np.log(np.maximum(p, 1e-300))
    x = z[:-1] - z[-1]
    return np.clip(x, -40.0, 40.0)


def resize_sequence(seq, m):
    p = normalize_positive(seq)
    m = int(m)
    if p is None:
        return np.ones(m, dtype=np.float64) / float(m)
    if m <= 0:
        return p[:0].copy()
    n = p.size
    if n == m:
        return p.copy()
    if n == 1:
        return np.full(m, 1.0 / float(m), dtype=np.float64)

    x_old = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    x_new = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, m)
    out = np.interp(x_new, x_old, p)
    out = np.maximum(out, 0.0)
    s = float(out.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        return np.ones(m, dtype=np.float64) / float(m)
    return out / s


def random_monotone_p(n, rng, kind=-1):
    n = int(n)
    if n <= 0:
        return None
    if n == 1:
        return np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)

    if kind < 0:
        kind = int(rng.integers(0, 5))

    idx = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64)
    t = idx / float(n - 1)

    if kind == 0:
        decay = float(rng.uniform(0.2, 20.0))
        p = np.exp(-decay * t)
    elif kind == 1:
        alpha = float(rng.uniform(0.1, 5.0))
        p = 1.0 / (idx + 1.0) ** alpha
    elif kind == 2:
        drift = float(rng.uniform(-5.0, 15.0))
        noise = 0.5 * rng.standard_normal(n)
        phi = drift * t + noise
        e = np.exp(np.clip(phi, -8.0, 8.0))
        a = np.cumsum(e[::-1])[::-1]
        s = float(a.sum())
        if s <= 0.0:
            p = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
        else:
            p = a / s
    elif kind == 3:
        split = int(rng.integers(max(1, n // 4), max(2, (3 * n) // 4 + 1)))
        hi = float(rng.uniform(0.5, 5.0))
        lo = float(rng.uniform(0.0, max(1e-6, hi)))
        p = np.empty(n, dtype=np.float64)
        p[:split] = hi
        p[split:] = lo
        if float(np.sum(p)) <= 0.0:
            p = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
    else:
        a1 = float(rng.uniform(0.5, 10.0))
        a2 = float(rng.uniform(0.5, 10.0))
        p = np.exp(-a1 * t) + 0.5 * np.exp(-a2 * t)

    p = normalize_positive(p)
    if p is None:
        p = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)

    return np.sort(np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64))[::-1]


def make_monotone_starts(n, nstarts, base_p, rng):
    n = int(n)
    nstarts = int(nstarts)
    starts = []

    if n <= 0:
        return starts
    if nstarts <= 0:
        return starts

    if base_p is not None:
        q = resize_sequence(base_p, n)
        if q is not None:
            starts.append(q)
            starts.append(np.sort(np.asarray(q, dtype=np.float64))[::-1])

    starts.append(np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n))

    if len(starts) < nstarts and n > 1:
        idx = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64)
        t = idx / float(n - 1)

        for decay in (0.0, 0.25, 0.5, 0.8, 1.2, 1.8, 2.5, 3.5, 5.0, 7.0, 10.0, 14.0, 20.0):
            if len(starts) >= nstarts:
                break
            p = normalize_positive(np.exp(-decay * t))
            if p is not None:
                starts.append(p)

        for alpha in (0.2, 0.5, 0.8, 1.0, 1.5, 2.0, 3.0, 4.0):
            if len(starts) >= nstarts:
                break
            p = normalize_positive(1.0 / (idx + 1.0) ** alpha)
            if p is not None:
                starts.append(p)

        for split_frac in (0.3, 0.4, 0.5, 0.6, 0.7):
            if len(starts) >= nstarts:
                break
            s = int(round(split_frac * n))
            if s <= 0:
                s = 1
            if s >= n:
                s = n - 1
            p = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
            p[:s] = 2.0
            p = normalize_positive(p)
            if p is not None:
                starts.append(p)

    while len(starts) < nstarts:
        kind = len(starts) % 5
        rp = random_monotone_p(n, rng, kind)
        if rp is not None:
            starts.append(rp)

    return starts[:nstarts]


def run_monotone_lbfgs(p, time_budget, tau_ratios=None, label=""):
    if tau_ratios is None:
        tau_ratios = (0.30, 0.10, 0.03, 0.01)

    p = normalize_positive(p)
    if p is None:
        return None, np.inf

    n = p.size
    if n <= 1:
        return p, fast_score(p)

    time_budget = float(max(0.0, time_budget))
    if time_budget <= 0.0 or not HAVE_MINIMIZE:
        return p, fast_score(p)

    best_p = p.copy()
    best_s = fast_score(p)
    cur_p = p.copy()
    cur_s = best_s
    t_start = time.monotonic()
    L = next_pow2(3 * n - 2)

    x0 = p_to_gaps(cur_p)
    M0 = cur_s / (2.0 * n)
    if not np.isfinite(M0) or M0 <= 0.0:
        return best_p, best_s

    tau0 = max(1e-15, float(tau_ratios[0]) * M0)
    teval = 1e-4
    try:
        tt = time.monotonic()
        monotone_f_and_grad(x0, tau0, n, L)
        teval = max(1e-6, time.monotonic() - tt)
    except Exception:
        pass

    bounds = [(-40.0, 40.0)] * (n - 1)

    for stage, ratio in enumerate(tau_ratios):
        rem = time_budget - (time.monotonic() - t_start)
        if rem < 2.0:
            break

        n_left = len(tau_ratios) - stage
        stage_budget = rem / float(max(1, n_left))
        if stage_budget < 1.0:
            stage_budget = 1.0

        tau = max(1e-15, float(ratio) * cur_s / (2.0 * n))
        maxfun = int(min(3000, max(60, stage_budget / max(1e-6, teval * 2.0))))

        options = {
            "maxfun": maxfun,
            "maxiter": max(1000, maxfun + 100),
            "ftol": 1e-12,
            "gtol": 1e-6,
            "maxcor": 10,
            "disp": False,
        }

        try:
            res = minimize(
                monotone_f_and_grad,
                x0,
                args=(tau, n, L),
                method="L-BFGS-B",
                jac=True,
                bounds=bounds,
                options=options,
            )
        except Exception:
            break

        if hasattr(res, "x") and res.x is not None:
            xn = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64)
            if xn.size == n - 1 and np.all(np.isfinite(xn)):
                pn = gaps_to_p(xn)
                if pn is not None:
                    sn = fast_score(pn)
                    if np.isfinite(sn) and sn <= cur_s + 1e-8:
                        cur_p = pn
                        cur_s = sn
                        x0 = xn
                        if cur_s < best_s:
                            best_p = cur_p.copy()
                            best_s = cur_s

        if label and stage % max(1, len(tau_ratios) // 3) == 0:
            print(label, "stage", stage, "score", best_s, "maxfun", maxfun, flush=True)

    return best_p, best_s


def run_free_lbfgs(p, time_budget, tau_ratios=None, label=""):
    if tau_ratios is None:
        tau_ratios = (0.20, 0.05, 0.01)

    p = normalize_positive(p)
    if p is None:
        return None, np.inf

    n = p.size
    if n <= 1:
        return p, fast_score(p)

    time_budget = float(max(0.0, time_budget))
    if time_budget <= 0.0 or not HAVE_MINIMIZE:
        return p, fast_score(p)

    best_p = p.copy()
    best_s = fast_score(p)
    cur_p = p.copy()
    cur_s = best_s
    t_start = time.monotonic()
    L = next_pow2(3 * n - 2)

    x0 = p_to_free_x(cur_p)
    M0 = cur_s / (2.0 * n)
    if not np.isfinite(M0) or M0 <= 0.0:
        return best_p, best_s

    tau0 = max(1e-15, float(tau_ratios[0]) * M0)
    teval = 1e-4
    try:
        tt = time.monotonic()
        free_f_and_grad(x0, tau0, n, L)
        teval = max(1e-6, time.monotonic() - tt)
    except Exception:
        pass

    bounds = [(-40.0, 40.0)] * (n - 1)

    for stage, ratio in enumerate(tau_ratios):
        rem = time_budget - (time.monotonic() - t_start)
        if rem < 2.0:
            break

        n_left = len(tau_ratios) - stage
        stage_budget = rem / float(max(1, n_left))
        if stage_budget < 1.0:
            stage_budget = 1.0

        tau = max(1e-15, float(ratio) * cur_s / (2.0 * n))
        maxfun = int(min(3000, max(60, stage_budget / max(1e-6, teval * 2.0))))

        options = {
            "maxfun": maxfun,
            "maxiter": max(1000, maxfun + 100),
            "ftol": 1e-12,
            "gtol": 1e-6,
            "maxcor": 10,
            "disp": False,
        }

        try:
            res = minimize(
                free_f_and_grad,
                x0,
                args=(tau, n, L),
                method="L-BFGS-B",
                jac=True,
                bounds=bounds,
                options=options,
            )
        except Exception:
            break

        if hasattr(res, "x") and res.x is not None:
            xn = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64)
            if xn.size == n - 1 and np.all(np.isfinite(xn)):
                pn = free_x_to_p(xn)
                if pn is not None:
                    sn = fast_score(pn)
                    if np.isfinite(sn) and sn <= cur_s + 1e-8:
                        cur_p = pn
                        cur_s = sn
                        x0 = xn
                        if cur_s < best_s:
                            best_p = cur_p.copy()
                            best_s = cur_s

        if label and stage % max(1, len(tau_ratios) // 3) == 0:
            print(label, "stage", stage, "score", best_s, "maxfun", maxfun, flush=True)

    return best_p, best_s


def multi_start_monotone(K, base_p, rng, time_budget, nstarts=None, label=""):
    K = int(K)
    if K <= 0:
        return None, np.inf

    time_budget = float(max(0.0, time_budget))
    if time_budget <= 0.0:
        return None, np.inf

    if nstarts is None:
        nstarts = max(6, min(32, 2000 // max(1, K // 16)))

    t0 = time.monotonic()
    starts = make_monotone_starts(K, nstarts, base_p, rng)

    scored = []
    for q in starts:
        rq = normalize_positive(q)
        if rq is None:
            continue
        s = fast_score(rq)
        if np.isfinite(s):
            scored.append((s, rq))

    if not scored:
        return None, np.inf

    scores = np.empty(len(scored), dtype=np.float64)
    for i in range(len(scored)):
        scores[i] = scored[i][0]
    order = np.argsort(scores)
    ordered = [scored[i] for i in order]

    best_p = None
    best_s = np.inf

    for i in range(len(ordered)):
        rem = time_budget - (time.monotonic() - t0)
        if rem < 3.0:
            break

        p = ordered[i][1]
        sb = max(2.0, (rem - 1.0) / float(max(1, len(ordered) - i)))
        lbl = (label + " s{:d}".format(i)) if label else ("s{:d}".format(i))
        p2, s2 = run_monotone_lbfgs(p, sb, (0.30, 0.10, 0.03, 0.01), lbl)
        if p2 is not None and np.isfinite(s2) and (best_p is None or s2 < best_s):
            best_p = p2
            best_s = s2

    rem = time_budget - (time.monotonic() - t0)
    if best_p is not None and rem > 10.0 and HAVE_MINIMIZE:
        p3, s3 = run_monotone_lbfgs(best_p, rem * 0.5, (0.10, 0.03, 0.01),
                                    (label + " polish") if label else "polish")
        if p3 is not None and np.isfinite(s3) and s3 < best_s:
            best_p = p3
            best_s = s3

    return best_p, best_s


def better_candidate(p, n_target, best_p, best_s):
    q = normalize_positive(p)
    if q is None:
        return best_p, best_s
    if q.size != int(n_target):
        q = resize_sequence(q, int(n_target))
    if q is None:
        return best_p, best_s
    s = fast_score(q)
    if np.isfinite(s) and s < best_s:
        return q, s
    return best_p, best_s


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2380.0, **kwargs):
    rng = np.random.default_rng(int(seed) % (1 << 32))
    t0 = time.monotonic()

    if budget_s is None:
        raw_budget = 2380.0
    else:
        raw_budget = float(budget_s)

    t0 = time.monotonic()
    deadline = t0 + max(0.0, raw_budget - 30.0)
    total_budget = max(0.0, raw_budget - 60.0)

    rem = deadline - t0
    if rem > 1600.0:
        N = 16384
    elif rem > 800.0:
        N = 8192
    else:
        N = 4096

    best_p = np.ones(N, dtype=np.float64) / float(N)
    best_s = 2.0

    print("search start N={} budget={:.1f}".format(N, raw_budget), flush=True)

    prev = None
    try:
        hs = np.asarray(height_sequence_1, dtype=np.float64)
        if hs.size > 0:
            prev = resize_sequence(hs, N)
            if prev is not None:
                best_p, best_s = better_candidate(prev, N, best_p, best_s)
                best_p, best_s = better_candidate(np.sort(prev)[::-1], N, best_p, best_s)
    except Exception:
        prev = None

    phases = [
        (64, 0.020, 30),
        (128, 0.030, 24),
        (256, 0.040, 20),
        (512, 0.060, 16),
        (1024, 0.080, 12),
        (2048, 0.080, 10),
    ]
    if N >= 4096:
        phases.append((4096, 0.080, 8))
    if N > 8192:
        phases.append((8192, 0.060, 6))

    for K, frac, ns in phases:
        K = int(K)
        if K <= 0 or K >= N:
            continue

        spend = total_budget * float(frac)
        remd = max(0.0, deadline - time.monotonic())
        if remd < 60.0:
            break
        if spend > (remd - 180.0):
            spend = max(10.0, remd - 180.0)
        if spend < 10.0:
            continue

        print("phase K={} budget={:.1f} best={:.6f}".format(K, spend, best_s), flush=True)

        pK, sK = multi_start_monotone(K, best_p, rng, spend, nstarts=ns, label="K{:d}".format(K))
        if pK is not None and np.isfinite(sK):
            if N % K == 0:
                exp = np.repeat(pK, N // K)
                best_p, best_s = better_candidate(exp, N, best_p, best_s)
            best_p, best_s = better_candidate(pK, N, best_p, best_s)
            print("after K={} best={:.6f}".format(K, best_s), flush=True)

    rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
    if rem > 60.0:
        spend = max(30.0, (rem - 60.0) * 0.60)
        ns = 8 if N <= 4096 else (6 if N <= 8192 else 5)
        pN, sN = multi_start_monotone(N, best_p, rng, spend, nstarts=ns, label="final")
        if pN is not None and np.isfinite(sN):
            best_p, best_s = better_candidate(pN, N, best_p, best_s)
            print("final monotone best={:.6f}".format(best_s), flush=True)

    rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
    if rem > 60.0 and HAVE_MINIMIZE:
        spend = max(20.0, (rem - 30.0) * 0.35)
        pf, sf = run_free_lbfgs(best_p, spend, (0.20, 0.05, 0.01), "free1")
        if pf is not None and np.isfinite(sf):
            best_p, best_s = better_candidate(pf, N, best_p, best_s)

        rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
        if rem > 30.0:
            spend = max(10.0, (rem - 20.0) * 0.30)
            pf2, sf2 = run_free_lbfgs(np.sort(np.asarray(best_p, dtype=np.float64))[::-1],
                                      spend, (0.10, 0.03, 0.008), "free2")
            if pf2 is not None and np.isfinite(sf2):
                best_p, best_s = better_candidate(pf2, N, best_p, best_s)

    rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
    if rem > 30.0 and HAVE_MINIMIZE:
        spend = max(10.0, (rem - 10.0) * 0.70)
        pp, ss = run_monotone_lbfgs(np.asarray(best_p, dtype=np.float64),
                                    spend, (0.05, 0.02, 0.006), "polish")
        if pp is not None and np.isfinite(ss):
            best_p, best_s = better_candidate(pp, N, best_p, best_s)

    final_p = best_p
    final_len = int(final_p.size)

    if N > 8192:
        cand = resize_sequence(best_p, 8192)
        if cand is not None:
            sc = fast_score(cand)
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc <= best_s + 0.005:
                final_p = cand
                final_len = cand.size
    elif N < 8192:
        cand = resize_sequence(best_p, 8192)
        if cand is not None:
            sc = fast_score(cand)
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc <= best_s + 0.002:
                final_p = cand
                final_len = cand.size

    candidates = []
    candidates.append(final_p)
    candidates.append(best_p)
    if prev is not None:
        candidates.append(prev)
        candidates.append(np.sort(np.asarray(prev, dtype=np.float64))[::-1])
    candidates.append(np.ones(1024, dtype=np.float64) / 1024.0)

    scored_candidates = []
    for cand in candidates:
        q = normalize_positive(cand)
        if q is None:
            continue
        s = fast_score(q)
        if np.isfinite(s):
            scored_candidates.append((s, q))

    if scored_candidates:
        cscores = np.empty(len(scored_candidates), dtype=np.float64)
        for i in range(len(scored_candidates)):
            cscores[i] = scored_candidates[i][0]
        corder = np.argsort(cscores)
        cscored = [scored_candidates[i] for i in corder]
        final_p = cscored[0][1]

    final_seq = None
    final_eval = np.inf

    if scored_candidates and time.monotonic() < deadline - 2.0:
        cscores = np.empty(len(scored_candidates), dtype=np.float64)
        for i in range(len(scored_candidates)):
            cscores[i] = scored_candidates[i][0]
        corder = np.argsort(cscores)
        cscored = [scored_candidates[i] for i in corder]

        for s, q in cscored[:3]:
            arr = np.clip(np.asarray(q, dtype=np.float64), 0.0, 1000.0)
            lst = [float(x) for x in arr]
            try:
                sc = evaluate_sequence(lst)
            except Exception:
                sc = np.inf
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < final_eval:
                final_eval = float(sc)
                final_seq = lst

    if final_seq is None or final_seq == []:
        arr = np.clip(np.asarray(final_p, dtype=np.float64), 0.0, 1000.0)
        final_seq = [float(x) for x in arr]

    return final_seq
```
